# Skills · A · part 28

848 entries, adform-media-plan-review to adlc-roadmap. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **adform-media-plan-review**: Media plan review for the Adform FLOW DSP. Inspect a pre-launch media plan: budgets, schedule, goals, and targeting, or review the candidate campaign tree it would generate. Trigger on "look at my media plan", "review the plan", "what optimisation recommendations does Adform suggest", "what's in plan X", "find my media plans". For the live campaign hierarchy use adform-campaign-structure; to forecast a plan's reach use adform-reach-forecast. Read-only. ([adform/agentic-skills](https://github.com/adform/agentic-skills/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/adform-agentic-skills/skills/adform-media-plan-review) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **adform-media-plans**: Media plan inspection, forecasting, and AI recommendations for the Adform FLOW DSP. List media plans, inspect a plan's line items, settings, forecast, or get optimisation recommendations. Trigger on "show me plan X", "forecast for this plan", "projected CTR or impressions or cookies", "budget scenario". For line-item detail use adform-line-items; for bid landscape on individual line items use adform-bid-landscape; for reach forecasting use adform-reach-forecast. Read-only. ([Abilityai/trinity-skills](https://github.com/Abilityai/trinity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adjust-playbook) · ★1)
- **adjust-pr-base**: Resolve the nearest open ancestor PR for drafting, or verify and correct an unmanaged PR base after creation, rebase, or a parent merge. ([sushichan044/dotfiles](https://github.com/sushichan044/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adjust-pr-base) · ★15)
- **adjust-remove-colors**: Localiser ou modifier la fonction "Suppression des couleurs" (effet manga/BD noir et blanc contrasté, distinct du niveau de gris simple), migrée dans la barre d'outils de la visionneuse principale (remove_colors_tool_qt.py). Utiliser dès qu'une tâche touche à settings['remove_colors_intensity'] ou à la réglette "Suppression des couleurs"/"Intensité". ([Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView](https://github.com/Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adjust-remove-colors) · ★2)
- **adjust-saturation**: Localiser ou modifier la fonction "Saturation" (formule PIL), migrée dans la barre d'outils de la visionneuse principale (saturation_tool_qt.py). Utiliser dès qu'une tâche touche à settings['saturation'], ImageEnhance.Color, ou à la réglette de saturation. ([Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView](https://github.com/Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adjust-saturation) · ★2)
- **adjust-scheduled-follow-ups**: Review and adjust future Billabex payment follow-up emails for selected customer accounts. Use to change a scheduled send date, recipient, subject, language, wording, or tone before an email is sent. ([billabex/claude-plugin](https://github.com/billabex/claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/adjust-scheduled-follow-ups))
- **adjust-screen-resolution**: Change the screen resolution so display content is clearer, larger, or better matched to the monitor. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-01-howto-main-digital-adjust-screen-resolution-3da5aa76))
- **adjust-sharpness**: Localiser ou modifier les fonctions "Netteté" et "Netteté adaptative" (Unsharp Mask) de MosaicView. Utiliser dès qu'une tâche touche à settings['sharpness'], settings['unsharp_radius'/'unsharp_percent'/'unsharp_threshold'], ImageFilter.UnsharpMask, ou la barre d'outils flottante de la visionneuse principale en mode sharpness/unsharp. ([Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView](https://github.com/Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adjust-sharpness) · ★2)
- **adjust-superwall**: Wire Adjust subscription tracking into Superwall purchase events via the useSuperwallEvents hook. Use when the project tracks purchases with Adjust and uses expo-superwall. ([Rocapine/rocalytics](https://github.com/Rocapine/rocalytics/tree/HEAD/plugins/rocalytics-setup/skills/adjust-superwall) · ★1)
- **adjust-table**: Adjust a working dlt SQL database pipeline for production — remove dev limits, add incremental loading, configure merge keys. Use when the user wants to remove .add_limit(), load the full table, or set up incremental loading on a cursor column. For speed/memory tuning (backend, chunk size, parallelism) use optimize-sql-performance instead. For inspecting loaded data or fixing column types/schema after a load, use validate-sql-database-data instead. ([dlt-hub/dlthub-ai-harness](https://github.com/dlt-hub/dlthub-ai-harness/tree/HEAD/workbench/sql-database-pipeline/skills/adjust-table) · ★65)
- **adjust-table**: Adjust a working dlt SQL database pipeline for production — remove dev limits, add incremental loading, configure merge keys. Use when the user wants to remove .add_limit(), load the full table, or set up incremental loading on a cursor column. ([cianfhoghlaim/cianfhoghlaim](https://github.com/cianfhoghlaim/cianfhoghlaim/tree/HEAD/dlthub-ai-workbench/workbench/sql-database-pipeline/skills/adjust-table) · ★4)
- **adjust-targets**: Review weight progress against the goal and change Prospre nutrition targets or the weight goal. Use when the user says their weight is stuck or moving too fast, asks whether to change calories, or wants new macro targets from a given day. ([prospre/prospre-plugin](https://github.com/prospre/prospre-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/adjust-targets))
- **adjust-text-tone**: Write or rewrite user-facing prose so it converts and doesn't read as AI slop. Use for landing/marketing copy, README and docs intros, onboarding and empty-state text, error and toast messages, release notes, emails, or when the user says "write copy", "improve this text", "make this punchier", "de-slop this", "sounds like AI", "adjust the tone", or "too wordy". Not for code, commit messages, or test names. ([korjavin/medicationtrackerbot](https://github.com/korjavin/medicationtrackerbot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adjust-text-tone))
- **adjust-training**: Use when a Gymlab member wants to change training they already have in Gymlab rather than start something new: swap an exercise in a saved workout for another (because of their equipment or their limitations, or because they dislike it), remove, reorder or re-target exercises, change sets, reps, weights or rest, group exercises into a superset or split one, rename a plan, change how many sessions a week a plan asks for, or make another plan the one they follow. Covers reading the workout first, finding alternatives that fit the member, showing the change and waiting for their confirmation, an… ([Pyrean/gymlab-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Pyrean/gymlab-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/adjust-training))
- **adjust-transparency**: Localiser ou modifier la logique de la fonction "Transparence" (rendre une couleur transparente par clic, zone/flood fill ou global) — outil "transparency" de la barre d'outils de la visionneuse principale. Utiliser dès qu'une tâche touche à transparency_tool_qt.py, à apply_transparency_click(), ou au panneau flottant flood/global/tolérance. ([Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView](https://github.com/Bruno-Aublet/MosaicView/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adjust-transparency) · ★2)
- **adjust-tv**: Mimes adjusting the TV, pointing a remote and clicking through channels or volume. Use when the command mentions change the channel, turn up the volume, use the remote, adjust the TV, or point the remote. ([sumitaich1998/slavex](https://github.com/sumitaich1998/slavex/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/adjust-tv) · ★1)
- **adjust-workers**: Scale up (or down) the number of concurrent workers on a Gas City rig. Use when the human adjudicator says "more workers on &lt;rig&gt;", "scale up &lt;rig&gt;", "I need more capacity for hecke", or when gc session list shows a rig has fewer active run-operators than open work items. Reads current session counts, proposes a max_active_sessions patch, and guides the briefed pack-change path (city-toml-via-packs-not-hand policy). ([tdupu/mathcity](https://github.com/tdupu/mathcity/tree/HEAD/subdomains/dev/skills/adjust-workers) · ★3)
- **adjusted-balance**: Verify updated account balances after posting adjusting entries ([bromso/metapowers](https://github.com/bromso/metapowers/tree/HEAD/plugins/accounting/skills/adjusted-balance) · ★1)
- **adjusted-mutual-info-score**: Compute the adjusted_mutual_info_score metric — provided by scikit-learn. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute adjusted_mutual_info_score, or asks how to score with adjusted_mutual_info_score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/adjusted-mutual-info-score) · ★3)
- **adjusted-mutual-information-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing clustering solutions (e.g., eeSNV- vs gene-expression-derived partitions of single cells) against known subpopulation labels and you need a chance-corrected agreement score over multiple bootstrap runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-mutual-information-clustering-evaluation))
- **adjusted-mutual-information-comparison**: Use when two cluster label vectors cover the same set of cells and you need a chance-corrected score of clustering stability, e.g. before vs. after removing CNV-harboring chromosome peaks from a scATAC accessibility matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-mutual-information-comparison))
- **adjusted-mutual-information-evaluation**: Use when comparing TLD/TAD partitions predicted by multiple algorithms (e.g., deDoc2, deTOKI, scHiCluster, Higashi) on downsampled, simulated, or single-cell Hi-C matrices at 40 kb binsize and you need an accuracy ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-mutual-information-evaluation))
- **adjusted-mutual-information-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking spatial domain detection or multi-omics clustering methods against ground-truth domain labels across multiple sections/replicates and a significance test of the ranking is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/adjusted-mutual-information-evaluation))
- **adjusted-mutual-information-partition-comparison**: Use when comparing two genome partition/TLD call sets — e.g., predictions from noise-perturbed vs. original downsampled Hi-C matrices — to measure how much domain structure is preserved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-mutual-information-partition-comparison))
- **adjusted-pvalue-significance-filtering**: Use when a pre-computed log2 fold-change and adjusted p-value table (CSV with gene-name column, per-comparison padj columns) must be filtered by significance and fold-change cut-offs to identify extreme gene entries across pairwise conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-pvalue-significance-filtering))
- **adjusted-pvalue-significance-quadrant-classification**: Use when a CSV of pre-computed log2 fold changes and adjusted p-values (per gene) must be partitioned into significant extreme/moderate up- or down-regulated quadrants across pairwise treatment-vs-control comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-pvalue-significance-quadrant-classification))
- **adjusted-rand-index-and-nmi-computation**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has ground-truth cluster labels (e.g. a simulated pancreas dataset with 7 true clusters) and you must score predicted cluster assignments against them, as in the SCISSORS comparison workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-and-nmi-computation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing predicted cell-type cluster assignments to known ground-truth labels for a scRNAseq dataset, e.g. benchmarking clustering pipelines on Zheng68K PBMC subsamples, Baron Mouse_1, or SC-Mixology datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-benchmark-evaluation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a spatial transcriptomics clustering method has produced per-spot domain labels and a manual layer/tumor annotation (e.g., DLPFC layers, tumor/non-tumor) is available as ground truth for ARI-based benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-benchmark-evaluation--1125a1892facb513))
- **adjusted-rand-index-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking clustering strategies on simulated scRNA-seq data with known ground-truth population labels, e.g. SymSim UMI datasets varying Sigma, alpha, depth, and rare-population proportion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-benchmark-evaluation--b5aef8047cf5d746))
- **adjusted-rand-index-benchmarking**: Use when comparing a predicted scRNA-seq clustering against known cell-type labels to quantify accuracy — e.g., benchmarking DESC, scVI, or other methods on datasets with reference annotations like the GSE115746 macaque retina bipolar-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-benchmarking--21b4549f1cfdd795))
- **adjusted-rand-index-benchmarking**: Use when comparing clustering accuracy of multiple single-cell methods on scRNA-seq datasets with known cell-type labels, and per-dataset ARI averages are needed to rank methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-benchmarking--8f41f1a052578c88))
- **adjusted-rand-index-cluster-comparison**: Use when spatial transcriptome and MALDI-MSI (or other spatial omics) sections have been aligned by competing methods and cluster labels exist on both sections, and you need label transfer AC/ARI to rank the alignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-cluster-comparison))
- **adjusted-rand-index-cluster-evaluation**: Use when you have scRNA-seq expression data with ground-truth cell-type labels and need a chance-corrected metric to compare clustering quality across imputation methods or against raw data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-cluster-evaluation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-cluster-recovery-benchmarking**: Use when comparing dimension-reduction or clustering methods on scRNAseq data that have published ground-truth cell-type annotations, and you need a single reproducible metric (ARI) to rank cluster recovery across datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-cluster-recovery-benchmarking))
- **adjusted-rand-index-cluster-validation**: Use when assessing whether repeated runs of an unsupervised clustering method (e.g., RCC with ConsensusClusterPlus/k-means base) yield consistent cluster counts and concordant assignments on bulk or single-cell expression matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-cluster-validation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-accuracy-computation**: Use when benchmarking clustering or spatial-domain methods against simulations that retain ground-truth cell labels, and you need a chance-corrected agreement score (ARI) per method per dataset, e.g. to reproduce the scSpace Fig. 1d-e comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-accuracy-computation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a task requires quantifying how well K-means clusters of imputed (or unimputed) log-transformed scRNA-seq expression matrices agree with known cell-type annotations via Adjusted Rand Index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-accuracy-evaluation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-agreement**: Use when comparing multiple clustering methods' assignments against ground-truth cell-type labels on single-cell RNA-seq data, and per-dataset ARI values must be assembled into a benchmark table (e.g. CASCC vs. baselines, SIMLR excluded). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-agreement))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-comparison**: Use when comparing cell-type label predictions from multiple reference-mapping or clustering methods against manually curated ground-truth annotations, and you need per-cell-type accuracy plus ARI summary metrics to build a comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-comparison))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing predicted cluster memberships from multiple clustering methods or simulation replicates against known/approximated truth labels and you need a chance-corrected accuracy score (ARI) per method per dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--f575ce2c35166693))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing unsupervised clusterings (e.g., Louvain on 50 PCs from different gene sets) against known reference labels (cortical layer annotations) to quantify recovery, such as benchmarking SVG-based vs. HVG-based feature selection on Visium DLPFC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--9eb5b6bb111df3ba))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when evaluating the quality of cell-type clustering results on scRNA-seq data against known cell labels, or when comparing clustering configurations (e.g., ablations, seeds, methods) via ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--c409e154de0a4560))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq clustering or batch-effect correction outputs against known cell-type labels (e.g., FACS-sorted labels), especially when assembling an ARI/silhouette comparison table across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--e68284262deb7f4b))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when clustering or community-detection predictions must be scored against known true labels (e.g. z_mus_dynamic_true from simulate_mus_dynamic_dcbm) across multiple subjects and time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--ab7e9cbd951fbceb))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing clusterings (e.g., Leiden community detection on a q-diffused, Gaussian, or scVI-derived graph) against expert manual annotations across cell subtypes or organ tissues, and you need an ARI-based accuracy metric for benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--ca1331f8bef4821b))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when a cell-classification or clustering output (e.g. scID target labels) must be quantitatively compared against published cell labels on the same cells, and a chance-corrected agreement score like ARI is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--f345f9bfe604903d))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when multiple cell-type annotation labelings of the same cells (e.g., scanpy Ingest transfer vs Leiden variants) must be scored against expert annotations to rank methods on a single dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-evaluation--41ab6a084857c10d))
- **adjusted-rand-index-clustering-validation**: Use when 2D cell embeddings (t-SNE or UMAP) have been generated at both original and scDEED-optimized hyperparameters and you must quantify whether optimized embeddings align 2D clusters with annotated cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-clustering-validation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-computation**: Use when scoring clustering quality of single-cell epigenomic methods against known ground-truth cell labels (e.g. sorted cell lines), comparing methods such as ChromSCape, SnapATAC, CisTopic, Cusanovich2018 and EpiScanpy on a shared dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-computation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-computation**: Use when comparing clustering methods on single-cell data where a ground-truth label vector exists (e.g., Splatter simulations with known cell types), and you need a chance-corrected agreement metric like ARI (often alongside purity) for downstream paired tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-computation--694c0a5e1fa05477))
- **adjusted-rand-index-computation**: Use when evaluating a cell annotation/clustering result against known ground-truth labels on scRNA-seq data, especially when comparing methods in Monte Carlo splits and needing a chance-adjusted agreement metric beyond raw accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-computation--665b73b6e2e1f2d7))
- **adjusted-rand-index-computation**: Use when benchmarking a single-cell clustering method (e.g. scDHA) against simulated ground-truth cell-type labels from sc_mosim/MOSim and you need a chance-corrected agreement metric over per-cell label vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-computation--d68c2b4c67b23f09))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when you have simulated single-cell data with known cell-type labels and a clustering result that must be scored for label recovery, e.g. benchmarking scDHA on sc_mosim output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-evaluation))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when a scRNA-seq or tabular embedding has been clustered (k-means, Leiden) and you need a label-based agreement score, such as comparing CCSF cMAP clustering against PCA or Leiden baselines on data with known ground-truth cell labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-evaluation--8e0eaed8f8a460f8))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has ground-truth cell-type annotations and you need a scalar score comparing predicted cluster labels against them, or when benchmarking clustering methods (e.g., scAce vs DESC vs scDeepCluster) via mean ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-evaluation--27fa9186b9540404))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when you need to score how well cluster structure in synthetic data matches a ground-truth reference clustering, e.g. after mapping synthetic cells onto original-data clusters in UMAP space across pilot sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-evaluation--a6ae4139096be76c))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when comparing cluster assignments (e.g., from K-means, hierarchical, K-medoids, SC3, or Seurat on GEM vs CNDM) against known cell-type or tissue-origin labels and a single external-validity score is needed, such as reproducing ARI = 0.73 on Tabula Muris. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-evaluation--25eae2a9b18b64bf))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when comparing clustering assignments from single-cell RNA-seq methods against known cell-type labels, or aggregating ARI across repeated runs to benchmark a clustering method against alternatives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/adjusted-rand-index-evaluation--9875dbcd855b3dd1))
- **adjusted-rand-index-evaluation**: Use when a clustering method's predicted cell labels must be scored against ground-truth cell type annotations on single-cell RNA-seq data, or when assembling a per-dataset, per-method clustering accuracy benchmark table.
